# Predictions before Pangram calls (E86)

Each Pangram call gets a prediction in its record before the call. This log scores them, so the audit has a hit rate. It started 2026-09-27; earlier drafts had no written prediction.

A row is a hit when the result's label matches the predicted one (Human means 100% Human; any AI share means AI), whatever the confidence. The 2026-09-29 emulate test's "hit (mostly)" is the one partial score, and it's marked as one. On 2026-10-02 (turn 15) a sweep found five rows scored by the confidence instead (turn 13's vow-line proposal and its diagnostic, turn 14's Physical Reminder rebuilds d1 and d3, and turn 15's "Notice it only promises…"), and they were fixed.
